<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="pt-BR" xmlns:mso="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office" xmlns:msdt="uuid:C2F41010-65B3-11d1-A29F-00AA00C14882">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>Busca de Materiais · F26 Componentes</title>
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=IBM+Plex+Mono:wght@400;500;600&family=IBM+Plex+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
  :root {
    --red: #EE1C24;
    --orange: #F78320;
    --yellow: #FEB914;
    --blue-deep: #0073B4;
    --blue-mid: #009DD0;
    --blue-light: #6DCFF6;
    --olive: #ACC32A;
    --green: #00A54C;

    --ink: #0F2430;
    --ink-soft: #52707D;
    --ink-faint: #8CA3AC;
    --paper: #F4F8FA;
    --card: #FFFFFF;
    --line: #DEE7EB;
    --line-soft: #EDF3F5;

    --font-sans: 'IBM Plex Sans', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, sans-serif;
    --font-mono: 'IBM Plex Mono', 'SFMono-Regular', Consolas, monospace;

    --shadow-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(15,36,48,0.05), 0 1px 1px rgba(15,36,48,0.04);
    --shadow-md: 0 10px 28px rgba(15,36,48,0.12), 0 2px 6px rgba(15,36,48,0.08);
  }

  * { box-sizing: border-box; }
  html, body { margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  body {
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
    background: var(--paper);
    color: var(--ink);
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    min-height: 100vh;
  }

  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    *, *::before, *::after {
      animation-duration: 0.001ms !important;
      transition-duration: 0.001ms !important;
    }
  }

  .brand-stripe { display: flex; width: 100%; height: 6px; }
  .brand-stripe span { flex: 1 1 0; }

  .topbar {
    background: var(--card);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
    position: sticky;
    top: 0;
    z-index: 30;
  }
  .topbar-inner {
    max-width: 1180px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: 16px 28px;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 20px;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
  .brand { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 14px; }
  .brand-mark { flex-shrink: 0; }
  .brand-text h1 {
    font-size: 1.28rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin: 0;
    letter-spacing: -0.01em;
    line-height: 1.2;
  }
  .brand-text p {
    margin: 3px 0 0;
    font-size: 0.76rem;
    color: var(--ink-soft);
    font-weight: 600;
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }
  .topbar-actions { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 18px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
  .meta-info { font-size: 0.76rem; color: var(--ink-faint); white-space: nowrap; }

  .btn {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: 0.85rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    padding: 9px 16px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1.5px solid transparent;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background .15s ease, border-color .15s ease, color .15s ease, transform .1s ease;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .btn:active { transform: translateY(1px); }
  .btn svg { width: 16px; height: 16px; }
  .btn-ghost { background: #fff; border-color: var(--line); color: var(--blue-deep); }
  .btn-ghost:hover { border-color: var(--blue-deep); background: #F0F9FD; }
  .btn-text { background: transparent; border-color: transparent; color: var(--ink-soft); padding: 9px 12px; }
  .btn-text:hover { color: var(--ink); }

  main { max-width: 1180px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 26px 28px 64px; }

  .search-panel { margin-bottom: 16px; }
  .search-box {
    position: relative;
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1.5px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    transition: border-color .15s ease, box-shadow .15s ease;
  }
  .search-box:focus-within {
    border-color: var(--blue-mid);
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(0,157,208,0.14), var(--shadow-sm);
  }
  .search-icon { position: absolute; left: 18px; color: var(--ink-faint); pointer-events: none; }
  #search-input {
    flex: 1;
    border: none;
    background: transparent;
    outline: none;
    font-family: inherit;
    font-size: 1.05rem;
    padding: 16px 46px 16px 50px;
    color: var(--ink);
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
  }
  #search-input::placeholder { color: #9FB2BA; }
  .clear-btn {
    position: absolute;
    right: 12px;
    border: none;
    background: var(--line-soft);
    color: var(--ink-soft);
    width: 28px;
    height: 28px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    line-height: 1;
    cursor: pointer;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
  }
  .clear-btn:hover { background: var(--line); color: var(--ink); }

  .results-meta { margin-top: 10px; font-size: 0.84rem; color: var(--ink-soft); padding-left: 4px; }
  .results-meta strong { color: var(--ink); font-weight: 700; }

  .table-wrap {
    background: var(--card);
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 12px;
    overflow: hidden;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
  }
  .table-scroll { overflow-x: auto; }
  table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 0.92rem; min-width: 640px; }
  thead th {
    background: #F7FAFC;
    text-align: left;
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    color: var(--ink-soft);
    font-weight: 700;
    padding: 13px 18px;
    border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--line);
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  thead th:hover { color: var(--blue-deep); }
  thead th.status-col { cursor: default; }
  thead th.status-col:hover { color: var(--ink-soft); }
  .sort-arrow { display: inline-block; width: 10px; opacity: 0.55; font-size: 0.62rem; }
  th.sorted-asc .sort-arrow::after { content: "▲"; }
  th.sorted-desc .sort-arrow::after { content: "▼"; }
  th.num { text-align: right; }

  tbody tr { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line-soft); transition: background .1s ease; }
  tbody tr:hover { background: #F5FBFE; }
  tbody tr:last-child { border-bottom: none; }
  tbody td { padding: 12px 18px; vertical-align: middle; color: var(--ink); }
  td.code { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-weight: 500; white-space: nowrap; }
  td.desc { max-width: 440px; }
  td.um { font-family: var(--font-mono); color: var(--ink-soft); }
  td.num { font-family: var(--font-mono); text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; white-space: nowrap; }

  mark { background: rgba(254,185,20,0.5); color: inherit; border-radius: 3px; padding: 0 1px; }

  .status-badge {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    font-size: 0.7rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    padding: 4px 10px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .status-badge.in-stock { background: rgba(0,165,76,0.12); color: #00713A; }
  .status-badge.in-stock::before { content: ""; width: 6px; height: 6px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--green); }
  .status-badge.out-stock { background: rgba(238,28,36,0.10); color: #C4141B; }
  .status-badge.out-stock::before { content: ""; width: 6px; height: 6px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--red); }

  .empty-state { padding: 64px 20px; text-align: center; color: var(--ink-soft); font-size: 0.94rem; }

  .toast-stack {
    position: fixed;
    bottom: 22px;
    right: 22px;
    left: 22px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-end;
    gap: 10px;
    z-index: 100;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
  .toast {
    background: var(--ink);
    color: #fff;
    padding: 13px 18px;
    border-radius: 10px;
    font-size: 0.86rem;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
    max-width: 360px;
    animation: toast-in .2s ease;
    pointer-events: auto;
  }
  .toast.error { background: #B0181F; }
  .toast.success { background: #00713A; }
  @keyframes toast-in { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(6px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

  @media (max-width: 720px) {
    .topbar-inner { padding: 14px 18px; }
    main { padding: 18px 14px 48px; }
    #search-input { font-size: 1rem; padding: 14px 40px 14px 46px; }
    td.desc { max-width: 200px; }
    thead th, tbody td { padding: 10px 12px; }
    .brand-text h1 { font-size: 1.1rem; }
  }

  :focus-visible { outline: 2.5px solid var(--blue-mid); outline-offset: 2px; }

  /* ========== Coluna de detalhe / linha clicável ========== */
  th.detail-col { width: 40px; }
  td.detail-col { text-align: center; }
  tbody tr.has-detail-row { cursor: pointer; }
  .detail-btn {
    display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
    width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--line); background: #fff; color: var(--blue-deep);
    cursor: pointer; transition: background .12s ease, border-color .12s ease;
  }
  .detail-btn:hover { background: #F0F9FD; border-color: var(--blue-deep); }
  .detail-btn svg { width: 15px; height: 15px; }
  .detail-tag {
    display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;
    font-size: 0.68rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: .02em;
    color: var(--blue-deep); background: rgba(0,115,180,0.08);
    border-radius: 5px; padding: 2px 6px; margin-left: 8px; white-space: nowrap;
  }

  /* ========== Modal ========== */
  .modal-overlay {
    position: fixed; inset: 0; background: rgba(15,36,48,0.5);
    display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
    padding: 24px; z-index: 200;
  }
  .modal-overlay[hidden] { display: none; }
  .modal-box {
    background: var(--paper); border-radius: 16px; box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
    width: 100%; max-width: 720px; max-height: 88vh;
    display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden;
  }
  .modal-header {
    background: var(--card); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
    padding: 20px 24px; display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; gap: 16px;
  }
  .modal-header-text { min-width: 0; }
  .modal-code { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.78rem; color: var(--ink-faint); font-weight: 600; }
  .modal-header-text h2 { margin: 2px 0 4px; font-size: 1.14rem; line-height: 1.3; word-break: break-word; }
  .modal-sub { font-size: 0.82rem; color: var(--ink-soft); }
  .modal-close {
    flex-shrink: 0; width: 32px; height: 32px; border-radius: 8px; border: none;
    background: transparent; color: var(--ink-soft); font-size: 1.4rem; line-height: 1; cursor: pointer;
  }
  .modal-close:hover { background: var(--line-soft); color: var(--ink); }
  .modal-body { padding: 20px 24px; overflow-y: auto; flex: 1 1 auto; }
  .modal-section { margin-bottom: 26px; }
  .modal-section:last-child { margin-bottom: 4px; }
  .modal-section h3 {
    font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .05em;
    color: var(--ink-soft); margin: 0 0 12px;
  }
  .modal-section-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 12px; }
  .modal-section-header h3 { margin: 0; }
  .btn-small { padding: 6px 10px; font-size: 0.78rem; }
  .modal-grid-2 { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 14px; }
  .field-label { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--ink-soft); }
  .field-label input {
    font-family: inherit; font-size: 0.92rem; color: var(--ink); font-weight: 400;
    padding: 10px 12px; border-radius: 8px; border: 1.5px solid var(--line); background: var(--card);
  }
  .field-label input:focus { border-color: var(--blue-mid); }
  .modal-empty-hint { font-size: 0.82rem; color: var(--ink-faint); margin: 0; }

  .gallery { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; margin-bottom: 8px; }
  .gallery-item { position: relative; width: 92px; height: 92px; border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden; border: 1px solid var(--line); }
  .gallery-item img { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover; display: block; cursor: zoom-in; }
  .gallery-item .remove-btn {
    position: absolute; top: 3px; right: 3px; width: 20px; height: 20px; border-radius: 50%;
    background: rgba(15,36,48,0.65); color: #fff; border: none; font-size: 0.85rem; line-height: 1; cursor: pointer;
  }
  .gallery-item .remove-btn:hover { background: rgba(238,28,36,0.9); }

  .drawing-view { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; }
  .drawing-preview-wrap { position: relative; display: inline-block; }
  .drawing-preview-wrap img { max-width: 100%; max-height: 320px; border-radius: 10px; border: 1px solid var(--line); display: block; }
  .drawing-pdf-box {
    display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 12px 14px; border-radius: 10px;
    border: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--card); font-size: 0.88rem;
  }
  .drawing-pdf-box a { color: var(--blue-deep); font-weight: 600; text-decoration: none; }
  .drawing-pdf-box a:hover { text-decoration: underline; }
  .drawing-meta { font-size: 0.76rem; color: var(--ink-faint); }
  .drawing-remove-btn {
    align-self: flex-start; border: none; background: transparent; color: var(--red);
    font-size: 0.8rem; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; padding: 4px 0;
  }
  .drawing-remove-btn:hover { text-decoration: underline; }

  .modal-footer {
    border-top: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--card);
    padding: 14px 24px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px;
  }
  .modal-save-status { font-size: 0.76rem; color: var(--ink-faint); }
  .btn-primary { background: var(--blue-deep); border-color: var(--blue-deep); color: #fff; }
  .btn-primary:hover { background: #005E92; border-color: #005E92; }

  /* Lightbox simples para ampliar imagens */
  .lightbox-overlay {
    position: fixed; inset: 0; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.8); z-index: 300;
    display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 30px;
  }
  .lightbox-overlay[hidden] { display: none; }
  .lightbox-overlay img { max-width: 100%; max-height: 100%; border-radius: 8px; }

  @media (max-width: 720px) {
    .modal-grid-2 { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    .modal-box { max-height: 94vh; }
  }
</style>

</head>
<body>

  <div class="brand-stripe" aria-hidden="true">
    <span style="background:#EE1C24"></span>
    <span style="background:#F78320"></span>
    <span style="background:#FEB914"></span>
    <span style="background:#0073B4"></span>
    <span style="background:#009DD0"></span>
    <span style="background:#6DCFF6"></span>
    <span style="background:#ACC32A"></span>
    <span style="background:#00A54C"></span>
  </div>

  <header class="topbar">
    <div class="topbar-inner">
      <div class="brand">
        <svg class="brand-mark" width="34" height="30" viewBox="0 0 34 30" aria-hidden="true">
          <polygon points="17,2 27,20 7,20" fill="#0073B4"/>
          <polygon points="7,20 17,20 12,29 2,29" fill="#EE1C24"/>
          <polygon points="17,20 27,20 22,29 12,29" fill="#FEB914"/>
          <polygon points="27,20 32,29 22,29" fill="#00A54C"/>
        </svg>
        <div class="brand-text">
          <h1>Busca de Materiais</h1>
          <p>F26 Componentes · Manutenção</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="topbar-actions">
        <span class="meta-info" id="meta-info">—</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main>
    <section class="search-panel">
      <div class="search-box">
        <svg class="search-icon" width="19" height="19" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65"/></svg>
        <input id="search-input" type="text" placeholder="Buscar por código, descrição ou unidade..." autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Buscar material">
        <button id="clear-search" class="clear-btn" type="button" aria-label="Limpar busca" hidden>&times;</button>
      </div>
      <div class="results-meta" id="results-meta"></div>
    </section>


    <section class="table-section">
      <div class="table-wrap">
        <div class="table-scroll">
          <table id="materials-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th data-key="codigo">Código <span class="sort-arrow"></span></th>
                <th data-key="descricao">Descrição <span class="sort-arrow"></span></th>
                <th data-key="um">UM <span class="sort-arrow"></span></th>
                <th data-key="estoque" class="num">Estoque <span class="sort-arrow"></span></th>
                <th class="status-col">Status</th>
                <th class="detail-col"></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody id="table-body"></tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div class="empty-state" id="empty-state" hidden><p>Nenhum material encontrado.</p></div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>

  <!-- ========== MODAL DE DETALHES DO MATERIAL ========== -->
  <div class="modal-overlay" id="modal-overlay" hidden>
    <div class="modal-box" id="modal-box" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="modal-title">
      <div class="modal-header">
        <div class="modal-header-text">
          <span class="modal-code" id="modal-code"></span>
          <h2 id="modal-title"></h2>
          <div class="modal-sub" id="modal-sub"></div>
        </div>
        <button class="modal-close" id="modal-close-btn" type="button" aria-label="Fechar">&times;</button>
      </div>

      <div class="modal-body">
        <section class="modal-section">
          <h3>Local de uso</h3>
          <div class="modal-grid-2">
            <label class="field-label">Equipamento
              <input type="text" id="field-equipamento" placeholder="Ex.: Compressor Atlas Copco 02" autocomplete="off">
            </label>
            <label class="field-label">Sub conjunto
              <input type="text" id="field-subconjunto" placeholder="Ex.: Unidade de filtragem" autocomplete="off">
            </label>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section class="modal-section">
          <div class="modal-section-header">
            <h3>Imagens do material</h3>
            <label class="btn btn-text btn-small" for="images-input">+ Anexar imagem</label>
            <input type="file" id="images-input" accept="image/*" multiple hidden>
          </div>
          <div class="gallery" id="images-gallery"></div>
          <p class="modal-empty-hint" id="images-empty-hint">Nenhuma imagem anexada.</p>
        </section>

        <section class="modal-section">
          <div class="modal-section-header">
            <h3>Desenho técnico da máquina</h3>
            <label class="btn btn-text btn-small" for="drawing-input">+ Anexar desenho</label>
            <input type="file" id="drawing-input" accept="image/*,application/pdf" hidden>
          </div>
          <div class="drawing-view" id="drawing-view"></div>
          <p class="modal-empty-hint" id="drawing-empty-hint">Nenhum desenho técnico anexado.</p>
        </section>
      </div>

      <div class="modal-footer">
        <span class="modal-save-status" id="modal-save-status"></span>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" id="modal-done-btn" type="button">Concluir</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="lightbox-overlay" id="lightbox-overlay" hidden>
    <img id="lightbox-img" src="" alt="Imagem ampliada">
  </div>

  <div class="toast-stack" id="toast-stack" aria-live="polite"></div>

  <script type="application/json" id="seed-data">[{"codigo": "49030", "descricao": "ROLAMENTO RIG ESF 1CARR 625-2Z", "um": "UN", "estoque": 2.0}, {"codigo": "51634", "descricao": "PARAFUSO CIL CL8.8 MG M4X 25MM", "um": "PÇ", "estoque": 141.0}, {"codigo": "51799", "descricao": "PARAFUSO CIL CL8.8 MG M6X 25MM", "um": "PÇ", "estoque": 108.0}, {"codigo": "52300", "descricao": "ARRUELA ALPARGATAS GK 3/4 X 1.1/2 X 6MM", "um": "PÇ", "estoque": 5.0}, {"codigo": "52718", "descricao": "ARRUELA LIS CIRC 267 CL8.8 M5", "um": "UN", "estoque": 229.0}, {"codigo": "52766", "descricao": "ARRUELA LIS CIRC   <script>
  (function () {
    'use strict';

    var SEED_FILENAME = 'MB52.htm';
    var SEED_DATE = '24/07/2026 22:31';
    var RENDER_CAP = 500;

    var DATA = [];
    var sourceLabel = SEED_FILENAME;
    var sourceDate = SEED_DATE;
    var sortKey = 'codigo';
    var sortDir = 'asc';

    // ---------- Persistência dos detalhes por material ----------
    // Guarda, por código de material: equipamento, sub conjunto, imagens anexadas
    // e o desenho técnico da máquina. Fica salvo no navegador (localStorage).
    var DETAILS_KEY = 'estoque_manutencao_f26__detalhes_material_v1';
    var detailsCache = null; // { [codigo]: { equipamento, subconjunto, imagens: [{id,data,name}], desenho: {data,name,type} } }

    function loadAllDetails() {
      if (detailsCache) return detailsCache;
      try {
        var raw = window.localStorage.getItem(DETAILS_KEY);
        detailsCache = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
      } catch (e) {
        detailsCache = {};
      }
      return detailsCache;
    }
    function saveAllDetails() {
      try {
        window.localStorage.setItem(DETAILS_KEY, JSON.stringify(detailsCache || {}));
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        // provavelmente excedeu o limite de armazenamento do navegador (imagens grandes)
        return false;
      }
    }
    function getDetail(codigo) {
      var all = loadAllDetails();
      return all[codigo] || { equipamento: '', subconjunto: '', imagens: [], desenho: null };
    }
    function setDetail(codigo, detail) {
      var all = loadAllDetails();
      all[codigo] = detail;
      detailsCache = all;
      return saveAllDetails();
    }
    function hasAnyDetail(codigo) {
      var all = loadAllDetails();
      var d = all[codigo];
      if (!d) return false;
      return !!(d.equipamento || d.subconjunto || (d.imagens && d.imagens.length) || d.desenho);
    }

    // Redimensiona/comprime uma imagem antes de guardar, para não esgotar o
    // espaço de armazenamento do navegador (limite típico ~5-10MB por site).
    function readAndCompressImage(file, maxDim, quality) {
      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        var reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onerror = function () { reject(new Error('Falha ao ler o arquivo.')); };
        reader.onload = function () {
          var img = new Image();
          img.onerror = function () { reject(new Error('Arquivo de imagem inválido.')); };
          img.onload = function () {
            var w = img.width, h = img.height;
            var scale = Math.min(1, maxDim / Math.max(w, h));
            var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width = Math.round(w * scale);
            canvas.height = Math.round(h * scale);
            var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
            resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality));
          };
          img.src = reader.result;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      });
    }
    function readFileAsDataURL(file) {
      return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
        var reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onerror = function () { reject(new Error('Falha ao ler o arquivo.')); };
        reader.onload = function () { resolve(reader.result); };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      });
    }
    function uid() { return 'img_' + Date.now().toString(36) + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8); }

    // ---------- Utilities ----------
    function stripDiacritics(s) { return s.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, ''); }
    function normalize(s) { return stripDiacritics(String(s || '')).toLowerCase(); }

    function formatQty(n) {
      if (typeof n !== 'number' || !isFinite(n)) return '—';
      var rounded = Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;
      if (Number.isInteger(rounded)) return rounded.toLocaleString('pt-BR');
      return rounded.toLocaleString('pt-BR', { minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 3 });
    }
    function escapeHtml(s) {
      return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
        return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
      });
    }
    function highlight(text, query) {
      text = String(text == null ? '' : text);
      if (!query) return escapeHtml(text);
      var norm = normalize(text);
      var q = normalize(query);
      var idx = norm.indexOf(q);
      if (idx === -1) return escapeHtml(text);
      var before = text.slice(0, idx);
      var match = text.slice(idx, idx + q.length);
      var after = text.slice(idx + q.length);
      return escapeHtml(before) + '<mark>' + escapeHtml(match) + '</mark>' + escapeHtml(after);
    }

    // ---------- Parsing: primary (grouped classic-list SAP export) ----------
    function tokenize(line) {
      return String(line).replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ').trim().split(/\s{2,}/).filter(Boolean);
    }
    function parseBrNumber(tok) {
      var t = tok.trim().replace(/\./g, '').replace(',', '.');
      var n = parseFloat(t);
      return isFinite(n) ? n : null;
    }
    function looksLikeUnit(tok) {
      return /^[A-Za-zÀ-ÿ]{1,3}[0-9]?$/.test(tok);
    }

    function pickMainTable(doc) {
      var tables = Array.prototype.slice.call(doc.querySelectorAll('table'));
      if (!tables.length) return null;
      var best = tables[0], bestN = best.querySelectorAll('tr').length;
      for (var i = 0; i < tables.length; i++) {
        var n = tables[i].querySelectorAll('tr').length;
        if (n > bestN) { best = tables[i]; bestN = n; }
      }
      return best;
    }

    function parseGroupedList(doc) {
      var table = pickMainTable(doc);
      if (!table) return [];
      var groups = Array.prototype.slice.call(table.querySelectorAll(':scope > tbody'));
      var scanGroups = groups.length ? groups : [table];

      var records = [];
      for (var g = 0; g < scanGroups.length; g++) {
        var trs = Array.prototype.slice.call(scanGroups[g].querySelectorAll(':scope > tr'));
        if (!trs.length) continue;
        var lines = trs.map(function (tr) { return tr.textContent || ''; });
        var firstTokens = tokenize(lines[0]);
        if (!firstTokens.length) continue;
        var code = firstTokens[0];
        if (!/^\d/.test(code)) continue;
        if (lines[0].toLowerCase().indexOf('total') !== -1) continue;

        var descricao = firstTokens.slice(1).join(' ').trim();
        var um = '';
        var estoque = 0;
        for (var i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
          var tokens = tokenize(lines[i]);
          if (!tokens.length) continue;
          var q = parseBrNumber(tokens[tokens.length - 1]);
          if (q === null) continue;
          estoque += q;
          var remaining = tokens.slice(0, -1);
          var umTok = '';
          if (remaining.length === 1) umTok = remaining[0];
          else if (remaining.length >= 2) umTok = remaining[1];
          if (umTok && !um && looksLikeUnit(umTok)) um = umTok;
        }
        estoque = Math.round(estoque * 1000) / 1000;
        records.push({ codigo: code, descricao: descricao, um: um, estoque: estoque });
      }
      return records;
    }

    // ---------- Parsing: fallback (simple one-row-per-record table) ----------
    function parseSimpleTable(doc) {
      var table = pickMainTable(doc);
      if (!table) return [];
      var rows = Array.prototype.slice.call(table.querySelectorAll('tr'));
      if (rows.length < 2) return [];
      var headerCells = Array.prototype.slice.call(rows[0].querySelectorAll('th,td')).map(function (c) { return normalize(c.textContent); });
      function findCol(patterns) {
        for (var i = 0; i < headerCells.length; i++) {
          for (var j = 0; j < patterns.length; j++) {
            if (headerCells[i].indexOf(patterns[j]) !== -1) return i;
          }
        }
        return -1;
      }
      var codeIdx = findCol(['material', 'codigo', 'cod.']);
      var descIdx = findCol(['texto', 'descri']);
      var umIdx = findCol(['umb', 'unid']);
      var qtyIdx = findCol(['utiliza', 'estoque', 'quantidade', 'qtd', 'saldo']);
      if (codeIdx === -1) return [];

      var records = [];
      for (var r = 1; r < rows.length; r++) {
        var cells = Array.prototype.slice.call(rows[r].querySelectorAll('td,th')).map(function (c) {
          return (c.textContent || '').replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ').trim();
        });
        if (!cells.length) continue;
        var code = cells[codeIdx];
        if (!code || normalize(code).indexOf('total') !== -1) continue;
        var descricao = descIdx > -1 ? cells[descIdx] : '';
        var um = umIdx > -1 ? cells[umIdx] : '';
        var estoque = 0;
        if (qtyIdx > -1 && cells[qtyIdx]) {
          var q = parseBrNumber(cells[qtyIdx]);
          if (q !== null) estoque = q;
        }
        records.push({ codigo: code, descricao: descricao, um: um, estoque: estoque });
      }
      return records;
    }

    function parseHtmlString(text) {
      var doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(text, 'text/html');
      var records = parseGroupedList(doc);
      if (!records.length) records = parseSimpleTable(doc);
      return records;
    }

    // ---------- DOM refs ----------
    var tableBody = document.getElementById('table-body');
    var resultsMeta = document.getElementById('results-meta');
    var emptyState = document.getElementById('empty-state');
    var searchInput = document.getElementById('search-input');
    var clearBtn = document.getElementById('clear-search');
    var metaInfo = document.getElementById('meta-info');
    var toastStack = document.getElementById('toast-stack');

    // ---------- Rendering ----------
    function getFilteredSorted() {
      var q = searchInput.value.trim();
      var qn = normalize(q);
      var list = DATA;
      if (qn) {
        list = DATA.filter(function (r) {
          return normalize(r.codigo).indexOf(qn) !== -1 ||
                 normalize(r.descricao).indexOf(qn) !== -1 ||
                 normalize(r.um).indexOf(qn) !== -1;
        });
      }
      var sorted = list.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
        var av = a[sortKey], bv = b[sortKey];
        if (sortKey === 'estoque') { av = av || 0; bv = bv || 0; }
        else { av = normalize(av || ''); bv = normalize(bv || ''); }
        if (av < bv) return sortDir === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
        if (av > bv) return sortDir === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
        return 0;
      });
      return { sorted: sorted, query: q };
    }

    function render() {
      var res = getFilteredSorted();
      var sorted = res.sorted, query = res.query;
      var total = sorted.length;
      var shown = sorted.slice(0, RENDER_CAP);

      if (!DATA.length) {
        tableBody.innerHTML = '';
        emptyState.hidden = false;
        emptyState.querySelector('p').textContent = 'Nenhuma base carregada. Envie um arquivo para começar.';
        resultsMeta.innerHTML = '';
        clearBtn.hidden = !query;
        return;
      }

      if (!total) {
        tableBody.innerHTML = '';
        emptyState.hidden = false;
        emptyState.querySelector('p').textContent = query ? ('Nenhum material encontrado para "' + query + '".') : 'Nenhum material carregado.';
      } else {
        emptyState.hidden = true;
        tableBody.innerHTML = shown.map(function (r) {
          var inStock = r.estoque > 0;
          var hasDetail = hasAnyDetail(r.codigo);
          return '<tr class="has-detail-row" data-codigo="' + escapeHtml(r.codigo) + '">' +
            '<td class="code">' + highlight(r.codigo, query) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="desc">' + highlight(r.descricao, query) +
              (hasDetail ? '<span class="detail-tag">&#128247; detalhes</span>' : '') +
            '</td>' +
            '<td class="um">' + highlight(r.um || '—', query) + '</td>' +
            '<td class="num">' + formatQty(r.estoque) + '</td>' +
            '<td><span class="status-badge ' + (inStock ? 'in-stock' : 'out-stock') + '">' + (inStock ? 'Em estoque' : 'Sem estoque') + '</span></td>' +
            '<td class="detail-col"><button class="detail-btn" type="button" data-codigo="' + escapeHtml(r.codigo) + '" aria-label="Ver detalhes de ' + escapeHtml(r.codigo) + '">' +
              '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="1"/><circle cx="12" cy="5" r="1"/><circle cx="12" cy="19" r="1"/></svg>' +
            '</button></td>' +
            '</tr>';
        }).join('');
      }

      var metaHtml = '<strong>' + total.toLocaleString('pt-BR') + '</strong> ' + (total === 1 ? 'material encontrado' : 'materiais encontrados');
      if (!query) metaHtml += ' · digite para refinar a busca';
      if (total > RENDER_CAP) metaHtml += ' · mostrando os primeiros ' + RENDER_CAP.toLocaleString('pt-BR');
      resultsMeta.innerHTML = metaHtml;
      clearBtn.hidden = !query;
    }

    // ---------- Sorting ----------
    var thElements = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('thead th[data-key]'));
    thElements.forEach(function (th) {
      th.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var key = th.getAttribute('data-key');
        if (sortKey === key) sortDir = sortDir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
        else { sortKey = key; sortDir = 'asc'; }
        thElements.forEach(function (h) { h.classList.remove('sorted-asc', 'sorted-desc'); });
        th.classList.add(sortDir === 'asc' ? 'sorted-asc' : 'sorted-desc');
        render();
      });
    });
    thElements[0].classList.add('sorted-asc');

    searchInput.addEventListener('input', render);
    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', function () { searchInput.value = ''; searchInput.focus(); render(); });

    function showToast(msg, type) {
      var el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'toast' + (type ? ' ' + type : '');
      el.textContent = msg;
      toastStack.appendChild(el);
      setTimeout(function () {
        el.style.transition = 'opacity .3s ease';
        el.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(function () { el.remove(); }, 300);
      }, 5000);
    }

    function updateMetaInfo() {
      metaInfo.textContent = 'Base: ' + sourceLabel + ' · atualizado em ' + sourceDate;
    }

    // ---------- Modal de detalhes do material ----------
    var modalOverlay = document.getElementById('modal-overlay');
    var modalCode = document.getElementById('modal-code');
    var modalTitle = document.getElementById('modal-title');
    var modalSub = document.getElementById('modal-sub');
    var modalCloseBtn = document.getElementById('modal-close-btn');
    var modalDoneBtn = document.getElementById('modal-done-btn');
    var modalSaveStatus = document.getElementById('modal-save-status');
    var fieldEquipamento = document.getElementById('field-equipamento');
    var fieldSubconjunto = document.getElementById('field-subconjunto');
    var imagesInput = document.getElementById('images-input');
    var imagesGallery = document.getElementById('images-gallery');
    var imagesEmptyHint = document.getElementById('images-empty-hint');
    var drawingInput = document.getElementById('drawing-input');
    var drawingView = document.getElementById('drawing-view');
    var drawingEmptyHint = document.getElementById('drawing-empty-hint');
    var lightboxOverlay = document.getElementById('lightbox-overlay');
    var lightboxImg = document.getElementById('lightbox-img');

    var currentCodigo = null;
    var currentDetail = null;
    var saveStatusTimer = null;

    function flashSaveStatus(msg) {
      modalSaveStatus.textContent = msg;
      clearTimeout(saveStatusTimer);
      saveStatusTimer = setTimeout(function () { modalSaveStatus.textContent = ''; }, 2200);
    }

    function persistCurrentDetail(successMsg) {
      var ok = setDetail(currentCodigo, currentDetail);
      flashSaveStatus(ok ? (successMsg || 'Salvo neste navegador.') : 'Não foi possível salvar (espaço do navegador esgotado).');
      return ok;
    }

    function renderGallery() {
      var imgs = currentDetail.imagens || [];
      if (!imgs.length) {
        imagesGallery.innerHTML = '';
        imagesEmptyHint.hidden = false;
        return;
      }
      imagesEmptyHint.hidden = true;
      imagesGallery.innerHTML = imgs.map(function (im) {
        return '<div class="gallery-item" data-id="' + im.id + '">' +
          '<img src="' + im.data + '" alt="' + escapeHtml(im.name || 'imagem do material') + '">' +
          '<button class="remove-btn" type="button" data-id="' + im.id + '" aria-label="Remover imagem">&times;</button>' +
          '</div>';
      }).join('');
    }

    function renderDrawing() {
      var d = currentDetail.desenho;
      if (!d) {
        drawingView.innerHTML = '';
        drawingEmptyHint.hidden = false;
        return;
      }
      drawingEmptyHint.hidden = true;
      if (d.type && d.type.indexOf('pdf') !== -1) {
        drawingView.innerHTML =
          '<div class="drawing-pdf-box">' +
            '<span>&#128196;</span>' +
            '<a href="' + d.data + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + escapeHtml(d.name || 'desenho-tecnico.pdf') + '</a>' +
          '</div>' +
          '<span class="drawing-meta">Documento PDF anexado.</span>' +
          '<button class="drawing-remove-btn" type="button" id="drawing-remove-btn">Remover desenho</button>';
      } else {
        drawingView.innerHTML =
          '<div class="drawing-preview-wrap"><img src="' + d.data + '" alt="Desenho técnico da máquina"></div>' +
          '<span class="drawing-meta">' + escapeHtml(d.name || 'desenho técnico') + '</span>' +
          '<button class="drawing-remove-btn" type="button" id="drawing-remove-btn">Remover desenho</button>';
      }
      var removeBtn = document.getElementById('drawing-remove-btn');
      if (removeBtn) {
        removeBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
          currentDetail.desenho = null;
          renderDrawing();
          persistCurrentDetail('Desenho removido.');
          render();
        });
      }
      if (d.type && d.type.indexOf('image') !== -1) {
        var img = drawingView.querySelector('img');
        if (img) img.addEventListener('click', function () { openLightbox(d.data); });
      }
    }

    function openModal(codigo) {
      var record = DATA.filter(function (r) { return r.codigo === codigo; })[0];
      currentCodigo = codigo;
      currentDetail = getDetail(codigo);
      // garante estrutura completa mesmo se vier de uma versão antiga salva
      currentDetail.imagens = currentDetail.imagens || [];
      currentDetail.equipamento = currentDetail.equipamento || '';
      currentDetail.subconjunto = currentDetail.subconjunto || '';

      modalCode.textContent = codigo;
      modalTitle.textContent = record ? record.descricao : '(material não encontrado na base atual)';
      modalSub.textContent = record ? ('UM: ' + (record.um || '—') + '  ·  Estoque: ' + formatQty(record.estoque)) : '';
      fieldEquipamento.value = currentDetail.equipamento;
      fieldSubconjunto.value = currentDetail.subconjunto;
      renderGallery();
      renderDrawing();
      modalSaveStatus.textContent = '';
      modalOverlay.hidden = false;
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }

    function closeModal() {
      modalOverlay.hidden = true;
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
      currentCodigo = null;
      currentDetail = null;
      render();
    }

    tableBody.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var btn = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.detail-btn') : null;
      var row = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('tr[data-codigo]') : null;
      var codigo = (btn && btn.getAttribute('data-codigo')) || (row && row.getAttribute('data-codigo'));
      if (codigo) openModal(codigo);
    });

    modalCloseBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    modalDoneBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    modalOverlay.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === modalOverlay) closeModal(); });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        if (!lightboxOverlay.hidden) { closeLightbox(); }
        else if (!modalOverlay.hidden) { closeModal(); }
      }
    });

    var fieldSaveTimer = null;
    function scheduleFieldSave() {
      clearTimeout(fieldSaveTimer);
      fieldSaveTimer = setTimeout(function () {
        currentDetail.equipamento = fieldEquipamento.value.trim();
        currentDetail.subconjunto = fieldSubconjunto.value.trim();
        persistCurrentDetail();
      }, 400);
    }
    fieldEquipamento.addEventListener('input', scheduleFieldSave);
    fieldSubconjunto.addEventListener('input', scheduleFieldSave);

    imagesInput.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      var files = Array.prototype.slice.call(e.target.files || []);
      if (!files.length) return;
      Promise.all(files.map(function (f) {
        return readAndCompressImage(f, 1280, 0.82).then(function (dataUrl) {
          return { id: uid(), data: dataUrl, name: f.name };
        });
      })).then(function (newImages) {
        currentDetail.imagens = (currentDetail.imagens || []).concat(newImages);
        renderGallery();
        persistCurrentDetail(newImages.length > 1 ? 'Imagens anexadas.' : 'Imagem anexada.');
      }).catch(function () {
        showToast('Não foi possível processar uma das imagens.', 'error');
      });
      imagesInput.value = '';
    });

    imagesGallery.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var removeBtn = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.remove-btn') : null;
      if (removeBtn) {
        var id = removeBtn.getAttribute('data-id');
        currentDetail.imagens = (currentDetail.imagens || []).filter(function (im) { return im.id !== id; });
        renderGallery();
        persistCurrentDetail('Imagem removida.');
        return;
      }
      var img = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('.gallery-item') : null;
      if (img) {
        var im = (currentDetail.imagens || []).filter(function (i) { return i.id === img.getAttribute('data-id'); })[0];
        if (im) openLightbox(im.data);
      }
    });

    drawingInput.addEventListener('change', function (e) {
      var file = e.target.files && e.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      var isPdf = file.type === 'application/pdf' || /\.pdf$/i.test(file.name || '');
      var task = isPdf ? readFileAsDataURL(file) : readAndCompressImage(file, 1600, 0.85);
      task.then(function (dataUrl) {
        currentDetail.desenho = { data: dataUrl, name: file.name, type: isPdf ? 'application/pdf' : 'image/jpeg' };
        renderDrawing();
        persistCurrentDetail('Desenho técnico anexado.');
      }).catch(function () {
        showToast('Não foi possível processar o arquivo do desenho técnico.', 'error');
      });
      drawingInput.value = '';
    });

    function openLightbox(dataUrl) {
      lightboxImg.src = dataUrl;
      lightboxOverlay.hidden = false;
    }
    function closeLightbox() {
      lightboxOverlay.hidden = true;
      lightboxImg.src = '';
    }
    lightboxOverlay.addEventListener('click', closeLightbox);

    // ---------- Init ----------
    function init() {
      // A base é sempre lida deste bloco, que é atualizado automaticamente
      // pelo script de atualização (ver AtualizarBase.ps1), e não mais por anexo manual.
      var seedEl = document.getElementById('seed-data');
      try { DATA = JSON.parse(seedEl.textContent); }
      catch (e) { DATA = []; }
      sourceLabel = SEED_FILENAME;
      sourceDate = SEED_DATE;
      updateMetaInfo();
      render();
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    else init();
  })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
